            (bn): BatchNorm2d(320, eps=1e-05, momentum=0.1, affine=True, track_running_stats=True)
          )
        )
      )
    )
  )
  (norm_head): LayerNorm((320,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
  (head): Linear(in_features=320, out_features=100, bias=True)
)
